Native reveal/picker success payloads may return user-selected server-side paths for local repair actions, but reveal failures use stable errors such as `path does not exist` instead of echoing resolved local paths.

When `serve` is blocked by incompatible local App state, recovery output leads with private archive, then app-scope reset preview and explicit `--yes` apply; it also offers a disposable `LOOPORA_HOME="$(mktemp -d)" <current Loopora CLI entry> serve ...` command for temporary Web preview or troubleshooting.
That disposable command must preserve the current CLI entry such as source-checkout `uv --directory <Loopora checkout> run loopora`, preserve the startup `--workdir` target when supplied, use a shell-quoted `<token>` placeholder instead of printing real auth tokens for token-auth network starts, avoid reusing the blocked App home after the temporary App-home assignment, and be framed as isolated state, not as migration, repair, deletion, or proof that the blocked App database is safe to overwrite.
Doctor includes that disposable action only when the Web target is not already blocked by port or bind preflight; otherwise it keeps archive, reset, and port/bind recovery as the executable next steps.

Supplied fields travel in a local URL fragment scrubbed after hydration. | Start/Fit guide modules, `first_use_review_actions.py`, CLI/tutorial output | Start/Fit guidance and browser Fit Guide checks | Review continuation treated as setup readiness, review context persisted or sent to the server, or incomplete-review setup |

First-use start port preflight: concrete CLI route output checks the default loopback Web port and aligns Web/doctor commands to an available alternate without starting Web. If no alternate is available, the Web route is blocked and the requested command is labeled unavailable; if other blockers remain, plain output labels the command as usable after blockers resolve. Start summary publishes route action ready/blocked kinds so JSON callers do not infer readiness from route presence alone. Plain payload projection remains ambient-port-independent unless the CLI enables preflight.
Embedded `fit_guidance.web_route_preflight` in start JSON mirrors the same port state, not only summary/action readiness, so automation sees one Web route truth inside the payload.
plain ready/blocked route summaries remain visible after the relevant target/review boundary is available.
Targetless `start` / `fit` prose points to a target-project rerun, not placeholder substitution.

Creation-choice route order: the Web creation-choice page hero and card order make Web conversation the primary default.
Creation-choice route order: same-Agent setup as the second path for users already in an Agent.
Creation-choice action visibility: each route card exposes its primary route action before the explanatory body text so first-viewport users see both the decision path and the next click, while the route order and same data attributes remain the contract.
Same-Agent setup route correction: `/same-agent` opens as a setup workbench, not a route-choice hero. Target and current host share the first viewport before readiness, install, or handoff; alternate create, Fit, and Support paths follow setup. Exactly one usable managed entry may restore its host selection; zero or multiple usable entries require an explicit host choice, and unselected adapters remain non-mutating alternatives.
`/tools` remains a compatible alias to that same surface; enhanced browsers canonicalize it to `/same-agent` while preserving query and hash state.
Fit Guide route correction: `/fit-guide` is the semantic Web entry for applicability judgment before setup or creation.
`/tutorial` remains a compatible alias to that same surface; enhanced browsers canonicalize it to `/fit-guide` while preserving query and hash state.
The `/support` route owns open-source help, redacted public-report guidance, issue/security routing, and workdir-scoped diagnostics copy actions; Same-Agent Setup may embed the same support panel and controller while only refreshing target-project state, but top navigation and error pages link to `/support` with workdir context so help is not discoverable only through Agent setup.

Fake alignment task predicates split across `executor_alignment_task_predicates.py`,
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_commercial.py`, `executor_alignment_task_predicates_data.py`,
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_operations.py`, `executor_alignment_task_predicates_product.py`,
and `executor_alignment_task_predicates_trust.py`.
Commercial task predicates split below the commercial compatibility facade across
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_commercial_metrics.py`,
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_commercial_payments.py`, and
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_commercial_billing.py`; cross-domain exclusion helpers live in
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_commercial_cross_domain.py`.
Data task predicates split below the data compatibility facade across
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_data_ingest.py`,
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_data_lifecycle.py`,
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_data_migration.py`,
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_data_read_models.py`, and
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_data_resilience.py`; cross-domain exclusion helpers live in
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_data_cross_domain.py`.
Product task predicates split below the product compatibility facade across
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_product_engagement.py`,
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_product_operations.py`, and
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_product_search_ai.py`; cross-domain exclusion helpers live in
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_product_cross_domain.py`.
Trust task predicates split below the trust compatibility facade across
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_trust_access.py`,
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_trust_governance.py`, and
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_trust_secrets.py`; cross-domain exclusion helpers live in
`executor_alignment_task_predicates_trust_cross_domain.py`.

Fake alignment domain spec scaffolds split across `executor_alignment_bundle_task_spec_scaffolds.py`,
`executor_alignment_bundle_task_spec_scaffolds_commercial.py`,
`executor_alignment_bundle_task_spec_scaffolds_data.py`,
`executor_alignment_bundle_task_spec_scaffolds_operations.py`,

`executor_alignment_bundle_task_spec_scaffolds_trust_governance.py`, and
`executor_alignment_bundle_task_spec_scaffolds_trust_secrets.py`.
Preset-to-appender spec-note routing lives in `executor_alignment_bundle_task_routing.py` as the module-level `TASK_SPEC_NOTE_APPENDERS` catalog instead of a per-call temporary map.
Append-only workflow note copy used by those scaffold bodies lives in
`src/loopora/assets/alignment/task-spec-workflow-notes.json`;
`executor_alignment_bundle_task_spec_notes.py` loads and validates that asset and owns predicate-aware append dispatch, locale selection, and idempotent heading checks.
Full commercial/finance, product/operations, and trust/security task-spec scaffold templates live in
`src/loopora/assets/alignment/task-spec-scaffold-templates.json`;
`executor_alignment_bundle_task_spec_scaffold_templates.py` loads and validates that asset and owns locale selection plus task placeholder rendering for commercial/finance, product/operations, and trust/security wrapper modules.

Fake alignment task-role fixture routing lives in `executor_alignment_bundle_task_roles.py`.
It maps task predicates to `task-role-fixtures.json` fixture keys through a module-level `TASK_ROLE_FIXTURE_REPLACERS` catalog instead of one bridge function per workflow family or a per-call temporary routing table.

Fake alignment task-workflow routing lives in `executor_alignment_bundle_task_workflow_dispatch.py`.
It maps task predicates to workflow replacement functions through a module-level `TASK_WORKFLOW_REPLACERS` catalog instead of rebuilding closure-based routing inside the dispatch function.

`src/loopora/assets/alignment/improvement-bundle-fixtures.yml`;
refund repair bundle fixture metadata, generated spec bodies, localized collaboration summaries, and workflow shapes live in
`src/loopora/assets/alignment/refund-bundle-fixtures.yml`;

`executor_alignment_bundle_refund_assets.py` acting as thin asset-specific wrappers so variant code does not carry the long fixture bodies inline.

`src/loopora/assets/alignment/bundle-scenario-fixtures.json`;

`src/loopora/assets/alignment/specialized-workflow-display-names.json`;
`executor_alignment_bundle_specialized_shell.py` loads and validates that asset instead of hardcoding display copy.

| Evidence manifest stable coverage refs | `check_results:<id>` verify refs may map to stable `done_when.<id>` coverage targets, while ad hoc `dynamic_checks` remain nonduplicated evidence detail/support signals for extra reproducible checks and must not create new required `done_when` targets. | `evidence_manifest_targets.py`, `evidence_coverage_targets.py`, `evidence_manifest.py` | evidence manifest tests, evidence coverage target tests | Promoting ad hoc dynamic check IDs into stable coverage targets, duplicating required Done When targets, or hiding check-result coverage behind freeform observations |

| Loop definition artifact preparation | Loop creation prepares local Loop definition artifacts before the Loop row is registered. | Loop definition artifact failure redaction | Local artifact-preparation failures report a stable loop-artifact-preparation error without permission details, server-local paths, or host exception text. Repository registration failures keep their original errors but still clean the prepared directory. | `service_run_registration.py` | run registration cleanup tests | Exposing raw local IO errors or leaving prepared directories after registration failures |
| Run start artifact preparation | Run start prepares local run artifacts before the Run row is registered. | `service_run_start.py` | run registration cleanup tests | Run rows registered before artifact preparation |
| Run start artifact preparation cleanup | If pre-registration artifact preparation fails with a local `OSError`, Loopora removes the newly created run directory, leaves no Run row/list entry, and reports a stable run-artifact-preparation error without permission details, server-local paths, or host exception text. | `service_run_start.py` | run registration cleanup tests | Leaving orphan run directories on pre-registration artifact failure or exposing raw local IO errors |
| Run start repository failure cleanup | Repository registration failures keep their original errors but still clean the prepared directory. | `service_run_start.py` | run registration cleanup tests | Prepared run directories left behind after repository registration failures |
| Run worker crash errors | Runner crashes persist a terminal failed Run, but local `OSError`s at the runner crash boundary project a stable local-runtime-access error into Run `error_message`, `run_aborted`, and terminal projections without permission details, server-local paths, or host exception text. | Alignment validation mirror failure boundary | After bundle lifecycle code has updated durable validation state, local validation artifact `OSError`s are diagnostic-only and must not block sync/import validation events or user-visible session state. | `db_alignment_records.py`, `service_alignment_artifacts.py`, `service_alignment_bundle_lifecycle.py` | alignment bundle lifecycle validation artifact failure tests | Local validation mirror failure as a user-path failure after durable validation state has changed |
| Alignment invocation stdout artifacts | Alignment event rows own executor event history. Invocation `stdout.log` is a local mirror of redacted executor messages for diagnostics: `codex_event` payloads are persisted before stdout mirroring, and stdout `OSError`s are diagnostic-only so live events, event listing, and executor completion are not lost to local log-file failure. | `db_alignment_event_records.py`, `service_alignment_invocation.py` | alignment invocation stdout mirror failure tests, executor event redaction tests | Treating `stdout.log` as the durable event stream or writing it before event persistence |
| Alignment worker crash errors | Alignment worker crashes persist a failed session, but local `OSError`s at the worker boundary project a stable local-runtime-access error without permission details, server-local paths, or host exception text. Task, transcript, agreement, and candidate files remain, and interruption can retry. | `service_alignment_recovery.py`, `service_alignment.py`, failure/retry projection, Web planning/Home | restart recovery API, projection, and static tests | Permanent Active state, killing live work, generic failure copy, duplicate task messages, or discarded judgment |

| Recent workdir mutation boundary | Read-only diagnostics/workdir-context probes do not write recents, and Loop refreshes keep non-Loop target shortcuts. | `settings_recent_workdirs.py`, workspace/service call sites, Web target surfaces | recent-workdir tests, Web Tools target tests, local asset diagnostics tests | Read-only probes mutating recent-workdir state or Loop refreshes deleting unrelated target shortcuts |
| Recent workdir diagnostic posture | Corrupt or unwritable recent-workdir state is a best-effort service diagnostic, not a terminal warning or first-use blocker. Load ignores invalid saved payloads while keeping valid entries. | `settings_recent_workdirs.py`, adapter setup surfaces, diagnostics surfaces | recent-workdir persistence tests, adapter install native-surface tests, diagnostics tests | Recent-workdir corruption blocking setup or terminal warnings for best-effort shortcut state |
| Recent workdir repair safety | Recent-workdir save preserves the previous file when replacement fails, and adapter setup can repair corrupt recent-workdir state without surfacing internal diagnostic log text to the user. | `settings_recent_workdirs.py`, adapter setup surfaces, diagnostics surfaces | recent-workdir persistence tests, adapter install native-surface tests, diagnostics tests | Partial recent-workdir writes or server-local paths in shortcut-state diagnostics |

## Service Boundary Inventory

Full module ownership notes live in `service-boundaries.md` so this main contract stays readable as the product and runtime boundary map. Architecture tests should read the combined design boundary when they need implementation ownership anchors; update the full service-boundary document when moving module responsibilities.
